世足賽訂單維繫營運，維持 2Q26 稅後 EPS 為 3.21 元之預估，
年增 28.4% 
聚陽 5 月營收 27.09 億元，月增 3.9%，年增 8.8%，營收達成率 65%，符
合預期，世足賽訂單貢獻維繫營運，維持 2Q26 淡季營收 81.77 億元之預估，
季減 8.1%，年增 6.3%，毛利率維持 23.8%，營業利益 10.77 億元，季減
21.8%，年增 14.4%，稅後 EPS 維持 3.21 元，季減 26.3%，年增 28.4%。 
預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 下修 5.6%至 4.09 元，但年增 17.6% 
3Q26 傳統旺季，客戶庫存偏低，回補庫存訂單需求維繫營運，惟美伊和談
進度干擾，品牌傾向短單急單的下單模式，預估出貨量由年增高個位數下修
至年增中個位數，預估 3Q26 營收下修 3%至 93.63 億元，季增 14.5%，年
增 5.3%，毛利率下修 0.9ppt 至 24.7%，營業利益下修 5.5%至 13.51 億元，
季增 25.4%，年增 23.7%，稅後 EPS 下修 5.6%至 4.09 元，季增 27.6%，
年增 17.6%。3Q26 營收及獲利仍維持年增格局，營運尚屬穩健。 
美伊和談變數干擾造成波折，但復甦方向不變，評價偏低 
展望 2026 年，聚陽的品牌客戶庫存陸續去化，回補庫存的訂單需求仍在，
日系客戶訂單穩健，可成長雙位數，來自 Dick’s Sport 訂單亦可年增雙位數，
Under Armour 訂單有機會年增 50%，Gap 旗下運動服飾品牌 Athleta 訂單
恢復增長，維繫營運基本盤，美伊和談變數造成波折，但復甦方向不變，預
估 2026 年營收 346.3 億元，年增 0.6%，稅後 EPS 為 15.45 元，年增 5.3%，
重啟成長，目前本益比 14.6 倍，在 14 至 24 倍歷史本益比區間下緣，評價
偏低，投資價值浮現，重申買進建議，目標價維持 310 元(20 倍 PER X 
2026 年稅後 EPS)。

元大觀點 
⧫  
毛利率優於預期且業外利益貢獻，1Q26 稅後 EPS 為 4.35 元，季增
23.5%，年減 15.5%，優於元大預期 8.8%。世足賽訂單維繫營運，維
持 2Q26 稅後 EPS 為 3.21 元之預估，年增 28.4%。 
⧫  
預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 反映 ASP 年減下修 5.6%至 4.09 元，年增
17.6%，估 2026 年稅後 EPS 為 15.45 元，年增 5.5%，恢復正成長。 
⧫  
美伊和談變數干擾造成波折，但復甦方向不變，本益比 14.6 倍，評價
偏低，目標價維持 310 元，基於 20 倍本益比、2026 年 EPS 評價。

營運分析  
毛利率優於預期且業外利益貢獻，1Q26 稅後 EPS 為 4.35 元，季增 23.5%，年減
15.5%，優於預期 8.8% 
聚陽 1Q26 營收 88.97 億元(原估 90.36 億元)，季增 12.3%，年減 10.4%，低於預期 1.5%，毛利率
僅季減 0.6ppt 至 26.1%(原估 25.3%)，年減 1.7ppt，優於預期，而費用控管好轉，營益率季增
2ppt 至 15.5%(原估 14.7%)，年減 0.4ppt，營業利益 13.77 億元(原估 13.27 億元)，季增 29.1%，
年減 12.8%，優於預期 3.8%，業外匯兌利益貢獻約 2800 萬元，稅後 EPS 為 4.35 元(原估 4.0 元)，
季增 23.5%，年減 15.5%，優於元大預期 8.8%。生產效率提升及產品組合較佳，帶動毛利率優於
預期。

世足賽訂單維繫營運，彌補戰爭變數干擾，維持 2Q26 稅後 EPS 為 3.21 元之預估，
年增 28.4% 
聚陽公布 2026 年 5 月營收 27.09 億元，月增 3.9%，年增 8.8%，以原預估 2Q26 營收 81.77 億元
(季減 8.1%，年增 6.3%)計算，營收達成率 65%，符合預期，營收月增，乃 4 月為季節交替傳統淡
季，5 月出貨逐漸轉旺。其中 5 月出貨量年增 6%多，匯率有利約 2%多，ASP 則年持平。來自世
足賽訂單貢獻維繫營運，彌補美伊戰爭變數干擾，維持 2Q26 淡季營收 81.77 億元之預估，季減
8.1%，年增 6.3%，毛利率維持 23.8%，營益率維持 13.2%，營業利益維持 10.77 億元，季減
21.8%，年增 14.4%，稅後 EPS 維持 3.21 元，季減 26.3%，年增 28.4%。2Q26 營收及獲利維持
年增格局，營運穩健。

美伊和談變數干擾，預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 下修 5.6%至 4.09 元，但年增 17.6% 
展望 3Q26 傳統旺季，客戶庫存偏低，預期回補庫存訂單需求維繫營運，惟最大變數仍係美伊 和談
進度干擾，油價維持高檔，引發通膨升溫排擠消費之擔憂，造成市場疑慮，品牌下單略顯猶豫，傾
向短單急單的下單模式，能見度不高，來自品牌客戶整體出貨規劃作調整幅度約 3%，預估出貨量
由年增高個位數下修至年增中個位數，而台幣匯率較 2025 年同期回貶，彌補 ASP 年減 3%之拖累，
預估 3Q26 營收下修 3%至 93.63 億元(原估 96.49 億元)，季增 14.5%，年增 5.3%，毛利率下修
0.9ppt 至 24.7%(原估 25.6%)，營益率下修 0.4ppt 至 14.4%(原估 14.8%)，營業利益下修 5.5%至
13.51 億元(原估 14.3 億元)，季增 25.4%，年增 23.7%，稅後 EPS 下修 5.6%至 4.09 元(原估 4.33
元)，季增 27.6%，年增 17.6%。3Q26 毛利率下修，係反映 ASP 年減，削弱部分獲利動能，不過
若客戶有急單挹注，聚陽仍有產能可因應，進而有營收及毛利率上修機會。 3Q26 營收雖下修，但
營收及獲利仍維持年增格局，營運尚屬穩健。

### Page 4

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：成衣 2026 年 6 月 3 日 Makalot 第4 頁，共 17頁
預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 上修 0.4%至 15.45 元，年增 5.3%
展望 2026 年，美伊和談進度不確定性恐影響 2H26 美國消費動能以及聚陽來自美系客戶的訂單展
望，不過品牌客戶庫存陸續去化，回補庫存的訂單需求仍在，只是尚待觀察戰爭發展，壓抑短期下
單意願，惟聚陽日系客戶訂單穩健，可成長雙位數，來自 Dick’s Sport 訂單亦可年增雙位數，
Under Armour 訂單有機會年增 50%，Gap 旗下運動服飾品牌 Athleta 訂單恢復增長，運動服飾訂
單需求維繫營運基本盤，美伊和談進度不確定性造成訂單復甦略有波折，但復甦方向沒有改變，預
估 2026 年營收下修 1.5%至 346.3 億元(原估 351.61 億元)，年增 0.6%，毛利率維持 25.2%之預估，
營益率略為上修 0.1ppt 至 14.6%(原估 14.5%)，營業利益下修 0.9%至 50.65 億元(原估 51.10 億元)，

年增 8.2%，稅後 EPS 上修 0.4%至 15.45 元(原估 15.39 元)，年增 5.3%。2026 年稅後 EPS 略為
上修係反映 1Q26 獲利優於預期。

### Page 5

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：成衣 2026 年 6 月 3 日 Makalot 第5 頁，共 17頁  
產業概況  
美國庫存偏低，回補庫存需求，有利 2026 年營運復甦 
近期美伊戰爭造成油價大漲，推升通膨預期，市場對於排擠消費的擔憂再起，而川普總統打壓油價
及抑制通膨，以推進降息的立場不變，且近期美伊和談進展，油價回落，舒緩通膨擔憂，後續須持
續關注美伊和談發展。我們對於 2026 年看法不悲觀，仍有一些兩個因素可以期待，其一為美國可
望啟動回補庫存，其二 2026 年世足賽可望誘發新的需求，提振 2026 年營運，以下個別分析這兩
因素。 
首先觀察美國服飾零售銷售表現，2026 年 4 月服飾零售銷售額 275.34 億美元，月減 1.5%，年增
5.8%，較前月年增 7.6%放緩，惟連 20 個月 YOY 正成長，銷售表現仍屬穩健。另一方面，美國服
飾庫存則相對平穩，2026 年 3 月服飾零售庫存略增至 584.58 億美元，月增 0.1%，年增 0.2%，庫
存動能低於 1H25 之 2-3%的年增表現。至於服飾存貨週轉天數增加 0.9 天至 63.7 天，仍低於 2025
年初 68-69 天，以及 2023 至 2024 年的 70 天水準，整體庫存偏低。搭配觀察美國紡織品進口
2025 年陷入衰退，而 2025 年年終消費旺季，服飾銷售維持熱絡的動能，將持續去化服飾庫存，預
期 2026 年回補庫存的機率大幅提升。 
圖 4：美國服飾零售銷售趨勢圖

資料來源：Bloomberg、元大投顧整理

### Page 6

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：成衣 2026 年 6 月 3 日 Makalot 第6 頁，共 17頁  
圖 5：美國服飾庫存及天數趨勢圖

資料來源：Bloomberg、元大投顧整理

世足賽商機擴大，聚焦美國再次舉辦且擴大參賽隊伍之效益 
另一方面在於 2026 年最重要的運動賽事焦點就是第 23 屆世界盃足球賽，此次世足賽兩大特點，
美國再次舉辦世足賽以及第三次擴大參賽隊伍，預期賽事熱度會大幅擴散。  
美國相隔 32 年再次舉辦，且是北美三國美加墨共同舉辦，盛況空前。1994 年美國第一次舉辦時，
現場觀賽人口總數由前一屆義大利舉辦的 251.6 萬人增加 42.6%至 358.8 萬人，創歷史新高，後來
各屆賽事都沒有突破這數字。全球電視收視人口總數由前一屆 10.3 億人倍增至 21 億人，年增
103.9%，FIFA 評估世足賽整體商機規模由 3.4 億美元增至 5 億美元，增加 47.1%，美國第一次舉
辦世足賽的效益相當顯著。 
圖 6：歷屆世足賽現場觀賽人口總數趨勢圖

資料來源：FIFA、元大投顧整理

### Page 7

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：成衣 2026 年 6 月 3 日 Makalot 第7 頁，共 17頁  
圖 7：歷屆世足賽電視收視人口總數趨勢圖

資料來源：FIFA、元大投顧整理

2026 年將是第三次擴大參賽隊伍。第一次是 1982 年由 16 支擴大至 24 支，比賽場次由 38 場增至
52 場，現場觀賽人口總數由 154.5 萬人增至 211 萬人，增加 36.6%，電視人口由 6 億人增至 7.5
億人，增加 25%，FIFA 評估的世足賽商機規模由 8 千萬美元增至 1.5 億美元，增加 87.5%。第二
次則是 1998 年由 24 支擴大至 32 支，比賽場次由 52 場增至 64 場，不過現場觀賽人口總數由
358.8 萬人降至 278.5 萬人，年減 22.4%，較不如預期，而電視人口則由 21 億人增至 28.8 億人，
年增 37.1%，FIFA 評估世足賽商機規模由 5 億美元增至 15.6 億美元，增加 212%。

至 104 場，比賽期間也會拉長，世足賽風潮將更熱烈，保守預估現場觀賽人口來到 550 萬人，年
增 61.6%，樂觀版應有機會突破 650 萬人。電視人口則可來到 80 億人，年增 60%，FIFA 預估商
機規模擴大至 110 億美元，年增 45.3%。預期在世足賽觀賽人口增加，商機擴大的帶動，對於足球
鞋及運動鞋，與足球相關服飾的銷售均有提振作用，提振成衣及製鞋族群於 2026 年重啟成長軌道，
仍可正向期待。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：成衣 2026 年 6 月 3 日 Makalot 第9 頁，共 17頁  
獲利調整與股票評價  
復甦略有波折，但復甦方向未變，2026 年重啟成長，評價偏低 
展望 2026 年，美伊和談進度不確定性恐影響 2H26 美國消費動能以及聚陽來自美系客戶的訂單展
望，不過品牌客戶庫存陸續去化，回補庫存的訂單需求仍在，只是尚待觀察戰爭發展，壓抑短期下
單意願，惟聚陽日系客戶訂單穩健，可成長雙位數，來自 Dick’s Sport 訂單亦可年增雙位數，
Under Armour 訂單有機會年增 50%，Gap 旗下運動服飾品牌 Athleta 訂單恢復增長，運動服飾訂
單需求維繫營運基本盤，美伊和談進度不確定性造成訂單復甦略有波折，但復甦方向沒有改變，預
估 2026 年營收下修 1.5%至 346.3 億元(原估 351.61 億元)，年增 0.6%，毛利率維持 25.2%之預估，
營益率略為上修 0.1ppt 至 14.6%(原估 14.5%)，營業利益下修 0.9%至 50.65 億元(原估 51.10 億元)，
年增 8.2%，稅後 EPS 上修 0.4%至 15.45 元(原估 15.39 元)，年增 5.3%。2026 年稅後 EPS 略為
上修係反映 1Q26 獲利優於預期。 
美伊和談的不確定性干擾聚陽來自美系客戶的訂單展望，惟考量 :1) 5 月營收 27.09 億元，月增
3.9%，年增 8.8%，動能穩健。2) 預估 2Q26 營收 81.77 億元，年增 6.3%，稅後 EPS 為 3.21 元，
年增 28.4%，維持年增格局。3) 預估 3Q26 營收 93.63 億元，年增 5.3%，稅後 EPS 為 4.09 元，
年增 17.6%，延續復甦。4) 戰爭造成訂單復甦略有波折，但復甦方向沒有改變，預估 2026 年營收
346.3 億元，年增 0.6%，稅後 EPS 為 15.45 元，年增 5.3%，重啟成長，目前本益比約 14.6 倍，
在 14 至 24 倍歷史本益比區間的下緣，評價偏低，投資價值浮現，重申買進建議，目標價維持 310
元(20 倍 PER X 2026 年稅後 EPS)。

公司簡介  
公司成立於 1990 年 1 月，是亞太地區知名的成衣製造商，主要業務為成衣的設計、製造及銷售，其客戶為全球及區域型之
大型成衣零售商，是全球第五大成衣代工商。 2015 年公司切入機能性服飾領域，運動服飾佔營收比重逐年提升。公司五大
廠區包括印尼、柬埔寨、越南、菲律賓、中國大陸，採「台灣接單，全球生產」的營運模式。公司成衣商品主要銷售地區
為美國市場，主要客戶包含百貨公司 (Kohl's JC Penney) 、大型賣場(Target、Wal-mart)、成衣專賣店 (Gap、Old Navy 、
Hanesbrands、Carter's、MANGO、Under Armour 等)；歐洲市場客戶有 ZARA、H&M 等，亞洲市場客戶包括台灣網購品
牌 Lativ、日商迅銷集團等。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：成衣 2026 年 6 月 3 日 Makalot 第14 頁，共 17頁

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 聚陽整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於低風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排名亦排行於領先的位置，
在奢華服飾行業中的公司中排名領先同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 聚陽的整體曝險屬於低等水準，略低於紡織品行業平均水準。紡織業中的公司通常較需要改善
的 ESG 議題包含人力資本、商業道德和人權供應鏈等。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：聚陽在針對重大 ESG 議題的管理水準和執行力為平均水準。公司採取了一些措施來
管理與重大 ESG 問題相關的風險，但這些措施不足以管理其所有風險。儘管如此，該公司尚未捲入任何具有重大財務
影響的風險事件。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

晶圓代工龍頭 7 奈 米 以 下 先 進 製 程 的 電 子 級 硫 酸 供 應 商 認 證 ， 成 為 全 球 唯 二 能 供 應 先

核心供應商 。 2028 年 台 灣 半 導 體 硫 酸 年 需 求 量 將 翻 倍 至 60 萬 噸 ， 預 期 公 司 在 龍 頭 廠

研 究 員 ： 吳 健 鉦

中 華 化 1Q26 營收 5.19 億 元 ， 毛 利 率 顯 著 回 升 至 18% ， EPS0.28 元 ：  
中華化 1Q26 營收 5.19 億元 (QoQ+3.80% 、 YoY+14.86%) ， 毛 利 率 顯 著 回 升
至 18% ， 遠 高 於 過 往 10%~11% 水 準 。 稅 前 淨 利 約 0.41 億 元 ， 稅 後 淨 利 為
0.36 億 元 ， 獲 利 水 位 已 回 到 三 年 前 高 點 。 毛 利 率 大 幅 改 善 的 主 因 在 於 產 品
組合優化，傳統基礎化學品比例降低，而高毛利的電子化學品與特用化學
品 比 例 持 續 拉 升 。 費 用 方 面 成 功 控 制 ，EPS0.28 元 ， 表 現 優 於 預 期 。 
03/2026 成 功 取 得 晶 圓 代 工 龍 頭 7 奈 米 以 下 先 進 製 程 的 電 子 級 硫 酸 供 應 商 認
證，成為全球唯二能供應先進製程硫酸的廠商，打破外商壟斷。聯電、力
積 電 與 美 光 等 客 戶 亦 積 極 爭 取 產 能。  
2028 年 台 灣 半 導 體 硫 酸 年 需 求 量 將 翻 倍 至 60 萬 噸，預期 公 司 在 龍 頭 廠 的 市
佔 率 預 計 將 達 到 48%~58% 。 目 前 的 產 能 規 劃， L04 產線 已 通 過 先 進 製 程 認
證 並 正 常 導 入 產 線 供 貨。L5 高 規 格 產 線 年 產 能 3 萬 噸，進 行 報 備 驗 證。L2 、
L3 產線 預計 2H26 完 工 改 裝 ， 將 優 先 供 應 非 龍 頭 客 戶 。L6 、 L7 、 L8 比照 以  
L4 規 格 複 製 以 加 速 驗 證 。公司 長 期 願 景 是 透 過 技 術 升 級 ， 由 傳 統 化 學 廠 轉
型 為 高 階 半 導 體 濕 式 化 學 品 與 軍 工 特 化 品 的 本 土 核 心 供 應 商。

產業速報

2 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係 「 首 次 」 給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時 ， 其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個
月目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為 四 個等級，定義如
上 。 爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」 或「區間操作」 之投資評
等，就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基
本 面 及 股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ，
研 判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。
我們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來
預測之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投
資 目 的 或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的 ，
既非對投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提
供詢價服務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值
及收益，可能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投
資 來 得 少 的 情 形 。

FIFA2026世足賽經濟效益商機可期
1資料來源：Statista、FIFA 、中信投顧整理
世足賽
百年來世界盃主辦國
• FIFA 世界盃(World Cup)由國際足球總會(FIFA)主辦，每 4 年舉辦一次，是全球規模最大、收
視率最高、影響力最深遠的單項體育賽事，其狂熱程度與吸金能力甚至超越奧運會。
• FIFA2026的參賽隊伍擴增至48隊(+50%)，總場次達104場(+62%)，將實質帶動球衣、足球鞋
及周邊裝備消耗量增長 。
• 上屆世足創造約170億美元經濟效益，本屆結合美洲主場強大消費力，相關品牌商機與營收規
模有望創歷史新高。而台股製鞋與紡織代工廠隨世足賽效益，帶動需求和拉貨力道提升。
歷年FIFA收入趨勢 (USD,B)

### Page 3

世足賽帶動旅遊、廣播、運動用品及相關消費需求
2資料來源： FIFA，中信投顧整理
世足賽
世足賽受惠行業經濟效益
• FIFA2026舉辦時間為2026/6/11/~7/19，由美加墨三國聯合舉辦，主辦城市高達16座，可望
帶動旅遊、飯店、房產、廣播、運動用品等消費經濟產業收入動能。
受惠產業 / 領域 經濟效益說明（核心亮點）
廣告和贊助 廣告商和品牌可以通過贊助世界盃賽事來提升品牌曝光度，吸引全球觀眾的注意。
體育賭博 世界盃期間體育賭博活動會大幅增加，這為賭博公司和相關機構帶來了龐大的收入。
旅遊業 世界盃吸引了大量球迷前往參加比賽，可為當地的旅遊業帶來了巨大的商機，包括住宿、餐飲、交通等。
電子商務 球迷會在賽事期間增加購買球衣、紀念品等商品，這為電子商務平台和零售商帶來了商機。
媒體和網絡平台 世界盃賽事的轉播權和網絡直播權是非常有價值的資產，媒體和網路平台可以通過播放賽事來吸引廣告收
入。
賽事觀眾收入 大量的觀眾會購買門票，這對主辦國的經濟有直接的影響。
基建城市發展 世界盃的舉辦通常需要大規模的基礎設施建設，這對城市的發展有積極的影響。

### Page 4

2026年世足賽贊助商
3資料來源： FIFA、各公司官網，中信投顧整理
世足賽
2026年FIFA主要合作夥伴及贊助商
• 2026年世足賽的贊助商體系主要分為三個級別：FIFA官方合作夥伴、世界盃官方贊助商以及賽
事支持商與供應商。麥當勞、樂事、百威英博等全球餐飲與零售龍頭依舊可見。
• 現代贊助不再只是在球場圍欄看板刷存在感，更強調數位變現與會員忠誠度計畫，各大贊助商
紛紛透過官方 APP 推出賽事預測、限定虛擬與實體周邊，甚至開放會員以點數兌換極其珍貴
的世足賽門票或頂級觀賽體驗，藉此深度綁定消費者。
第一級：FIFA 合作夥伴 第二級：世界盃官方贊助商 第三級：區域官方支持者
最頂級全球合作夥伴，享
有國際足聯旗下所有賽事
與推廣項目的獨家權益。
本屆世界盃專屬全球合作夥伴，圍
繞美加墨世界盃大賽展開全方位的
商業推廣與曝光。
專注於各大洲或賽區當地的市場營銷與本地
支持。

敬鵬 (2355 TT)

毛利率表現遇壓

維持中立評等，目標價 55 元。 
1. 車用回温及工業成長，維持 2026 年營收成長看法，估計 14%。 
2. 自結 4 月 EPS 0.13 元，保守看待 2Q26 毛利率表現。 
3. 通訊及航天營收比重下滑；估計泰國新廠最快 1H27 產出貢獻。

車用回温及工業成長，維持 2026 年營收成長看法，估計 14% 
歷經 4 年營收下滑後，公司維持年初對今年營收成長正向看法不變，其中成長動能
有二，一為油車需求好轉，公司表示原對車用產品需求成長 保守看待，唯今年歐、
美有些車廠又轉向油車發展動作，因此訂單水準及需求相對先前呈現好轉。二則是
工業相關新產品成長。整體來看，公司對 2026 年營收展望正向，至於成長幅度取
決於汽車市場回溫速度與工業電子新產品訂單進來的速度， 富邦估計年成長14%。

自結 4 月 EPS 0.13 元，保守看待 2Q26 毛利率表現 
1Q26 合併營收 40.56 億元 ，QOQ+1.6%，YOY+5.6%， 符合預期 ， 毛利率12.1%，
較前一季 14.0%及同期 12.6%呈現下滑，主要是受原物料上漲干擾，而費用控制得
宜，加上匯兌利益挹注，稅後淨利 2.47 億元，EPS 0.62 元。另 1Q26 用產品中，
車用需求回溫 ，1Q26 汽車佔由 75%提升至 77%， 另需求看俏的工業應用佔比維持
14%，通訊/航天比重由 6%進一步下滑至 5%，消費也由 5%下滑至 4%。此外，公
司自結 4 月單月 EPS 0.13 元，低於市場預期，主要是原料成本持續墊高，由於目前
漲價是與客戶成本分攤，幅度尚無法 100%完全轉嫁成本上漲，保守看待 2Q26 毛
利率表現，而未來不排除二度漲價。

通訊及航天營收比重下滑；估計泰國新廠最快 1H27 產出貢獻 
公司通訊及航天比重由 2024 年 9%，下滑至 2025 的 6%，1Q26 進一步下滑至
5%，公司表示，通訊及航天客戶目前出貨客戶非 SpaceX，且提供產品以較利基品
項為主，營收貢獻不大；至於泰國廠方面，1Q26 虧損 300 萬，主要因素有舊廠翻
新對產能、人力有影響，加上原物料成本上漲且多半進口，預期下半年營運及獲利
將改善，包括來自漲價效益。另泰國新廠方面，預計 2026 年底前完工，該廠預計
投資 30 億元，完成後最大產值約 30~40 億元，預計 1H27 產出貢獻，而該廠非單
一客戶專廠，產品應用也非單一。

富邦不同之處 
富邦為少數持續追蹤且出具更新報告券商之一。

評價與風險 
受惠車用市場需求回温及工業需求成長，營收展望正向，配合 LEO 題材，公司今年
來股價大漲，由 30 元一度上漲至最高 71 元，目前 P/E 29.6 倍及 P/B 1.4 倍，評價
來到歷史區間上緣，考量獲利受原物料干擾，加上LEO 佔比相對有限，短期評價上
調空間相對有限，因此暫維持中立評等，目標價則反應大盤上漲及同業水準上調至
55 元，約相當 2026 年的 1.24 倍 P/B。

富邦的股票評等標準 
評等  定義 
買進 預估未來6個月內的絕對報酬超過15%  
中立  預估未來6個月內有絕對報酬介於15%與負15%之間 
賣出 預估未來6個月內的絕對報酬高於負15% 
未評等 由於富邦目前與該公司有特定交易或沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等  
評估中 目前正在研議個股的投資評等，將於3到6個月內提供投資評等

產業評等 定義 
優於大盤 預估該產業在未來6個月內會比大盤指數表現突出 
持平 預估該產業在未來6個月內與大盤指數表現相較持平 
劣於大盤 預估該產業在未來6個月內會比大盤指數表現較差

Fubon's Rating System 
Rating Definition 
Buy Expected absolute return to be over 15% within the next 6 months  
Neutral  Expected absolute return to be level within the next 6 months  
Sell  Expected absolute return to be over negative 15% within the next 6 months 
Not Rated (NR)  Pursuant to Fubon acting in deals involving the company or there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for determining an investment rating  
Under Review Fubon is in the process of determining an investment rating and will assign a rating within the 3- to 6-month horizon

Sector Rating Definition 
Overweight Sector expected to outperform the broader market indexes within the next 6-12 months 
Market Weight  Sector expected to perform in line with the broader market averages within the next 6-12 months 
Underweight  Sector expected to underperform the broader market indexes within the next 6-12 months.

股價相對大盤走勢

南亞科 (2408 TT)  Trading Buy

系統性風險，調降至 Trading Buy 。  
投資建議 ： Kioxia(258A JP) 、 SanDisk(SNDK US) 、 Solidigm 及
Cisco(CSCO US) 認購南亞科私募普通股，對南亞科而言，此為
具策略意義的合作，除挹注資金外，亦有助於綁定長期客戶需
求，並提升拓展未來 CSP 等大客戶的機會。供給端方面， DRAM
大廠持續將資本支出與產能轉向 HBM 與先進製程 DDR5，壓縮
傳統 DDR4 與 LPDDR4 供給；需求端受惠 AI 伺服器與邊緣運
算帶動記憶體搭載量提升，支撐整體 DRAM 報價維持高檔。在
價格上行帶動下，預估南亞科 2026 年稅後 EPS 達 54.47 元。過
去 3 個月記憶體產業面臨多項利空雜音，包括現貨價格波動、
中國供給擴張疑慮、終端需求放緩，及近期市場對 NVIDIA Vera
平台 SOCAMM2 配置調整的擔憂。然而， DRAM 及 NAND 產品
報價持續上漲，顯示產業供需結構及基本面未出現明顯轉弱跡
象。考量短期市場波動加劇，因此將投資評等調降至 Trading 
Buy ，待市場情緒及籌碼結構趨穩後再重新檢視投資機會。  
1Q26 稅後 EPS 7.55 元：1Q26 營收為 490.87 億元 ，QoQ+63.11% ，
南亞科 DRAM 平均銷售價格較 4Q25 增加 ，ASP 約 QoQ+>70% 。
匯率對營收影響 QoQ+1~+3% ，Shipment QoQ-4~-6% 。毛利率受
Blend ASP 上升與匯率有利影響 ， 1Q26 毛利率從 4Q25 的 49.04%
上揚至 67.87% ，增加 18.83 個百分點。 1Q26 稅後 EPS 7.55 元，
淨值 62.25 元。  
2026 年資本支出提升至 520 億元 ：南亞科董事會通過 2026 年
的資本支出預算以不超過新台幣 520 億元為上限， YoY+287% ，
其中 62 億元是 2025 年遞延至 2026 年，資本支出中 70% 用在新
廠的廠務無塵室等，剩下 30% 則會使用在設備採購上。預估新
廠將在 2027 年初裝機， 1H27 正式量產，規劃是 1H28 可以達到
月產能 15~20K 片 /月的目標，以 DDR5 為主。目前 DDR5 占南
亞科約 10% ，公司目標是維持在 10% 以上並逐步提升，但仍需
視整體缺貨結構而定。

  南亞科 ESG ：  
佈局綠色科技生產  
DRAM 為智慧世代的關鍵零組件，南亞科研發先進且具高效能的 「環境友善
產品」以符合市場與客戶需求。所有產品於設計初期時，即導入生命週期思
維與綠色設計，透過綠色產品推動委員會進行綠色產品管理。

重視人才發展與培養  
「人才」是公司創新、營運與永續發展的根本，強化公司全方位人才培育與
發展，南亞科技與長庚大學簽署成立記憶體學程碩士班，學生除可獲高額獎
學金，更可獲得畢業後的就業機會。南亞科技期盼從育才、用才、引才、聚
才、留才各方面，強化臺灣記憶體產業在國際上的競爭力。

共同攜手永續夥伴  
南亞科攜手上下游合作夥伴一同前行， 295 家供應商認同公司的永續理念，
一同簽署南亞科技企業社會責任承諾書且簽署率達 100% 、輔導供應商進行
永續改善，稽核、輔導與改善率均 100% 完成，並持續辦理供應鏈研討會。
此外，也與供應商共同合作推動人權及環境相關的議題。

永續績效備受肯定  
南亞科視 ESG 為企業的重要承諾，南亞科追求經營績效的同時，也同步落
實 ESG 的平衡發展。南亞科技參加 2025 第 18 屆台灣企業永續獎評選，公
司已連續第九年獲頒多項殊榮，獲獎包括年度最佳報告 (製造業 )、台灣 100
大永續典範企業獎及四項永續單項績效  – 氣候領袖獎、創新成長領袖獎、
資訊安全領袖獎與透明誠信領袖獎。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

京元電子 (2449 TT)  Trading Buy

短期 AI 半導體受系統性風險影響，調降至 Trading Buy 。 
投資建議 ：京元電憑藉自製預燒爐 （ Burn-in ）的優勢，在 AI GPU 
成品測試市場具領導地位 。隨著新一代  Rubin 晶片架構更複雜
且功耗 （TDP ） 大增，其測試時間預計將較前一代  Blackwell 增
加 50% 以上，帶動測試單價大幅提升。另隨著新款  ASIC 功耗
提升 ， 客戶預計將開始導入高單價 Burn-in 與系統級測試 （ SLT ），
這將使 ASIC 的最測試時間倍增，成為極具潛力的營收引擎。
預估京元電 2026 年稅後 EPS 9.85 元。 AI 加速器、高速運算及
先進封裝測試需求持續成長，公司仍受惠於 AI 晶片測試內容增
加及產能擴充，中長期成長趨勢未變。然近期全球半導體類股
修正幅度擴大，高評價 AI 供應鏈面臨賣壓，短期股價表現可能
受資金及籌碼面影響，因此將投資評等調降至  Trading Buy 。  
預估 1Q26 稅後 EPS 1.87 元：京元電受惠於 CPU、 ASIC 客戶，
以及台積電晶圓測試委外與智慧型手機客戶需求優於預期。
1Q26 營收為 101.92 億元， QoQ+2.27% ，毛利率隨著產能利用率
而提升，預估毛利率將上揚 0.5 個百分點左右，稅後 EPS 1.87
元。  
資本支出大幅調升至 500 億元 ：京元電 2026 年資本支出從原
393.72 億元調升至 500 億元，用於建置無塵室及高功率燒機測
試設備，預計產能擴充 30%-50% ，目前頭份及楊梅新廠區建設
中， 2H26 投產貢獻營收。公司規劃 2026 年量產廠區將增至 11
座，集中於苗栗頭份、桃園楊梅及新加坡。 2027 年主要擴產重
點在苗栗銅鑼 P4 與苗栗竹南 P6-P7 ，最快 1H27 量產貢獻營收。
京元電資本支出非常積極，將略高於 2026 年的全年營收。資金
來源主要為自有資金、銀行融資及資本化租賃，後者已核准 100
億元用於高階設備取得 。 另股利大幅下滑 ， 總股利為每股1.5 元，
含現金股利 1 元及股票股利 0.5 元 。 折舊壓力將於2026-2027 年
浮現，短期內固定成本上升，不過新產能貢獻可望提升營收與
現金流入，仍要注意景氣波動時獲利和現金流更敏感。

2 群益投顧 
  京元電 ESG ：  
京元電積極推動  ESG （環境、社會、公司治理）相關政策與行動，並於官
方網站設有專屬  ESG 頁面與歷年永續報告書 ， 公開揭露永續發展成果與目
標。  
環境（ E）  
公司設定科學基礎減碳目標： 2025 年範疇 1+2 排放較 2022 年減 10%、2030
年減 15% 、 2040 年減 30% 、 2050 年減 84% ；強化廢棄物零填埋及屋頂光電
推進。

社會（ S）  
強調員工安全與供應鏈管理 ， 達成 100% 衝突礦產調查及高風險供應商稽核 ；
ESG 評級達 B+，顯示社會責任穩健。

治理（ G）  
完善風險管理機制，將 ESG 視為永續動力；參照 GRI、SASB 及 TCFD 編製
報告，強化利害關係人溝通。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

義隆(2458)

Price(06/08):156.5 
Target Price：210

上半年客戶的急單讓營收呈現淡季不淡，同時 AI 影像辨識產品的
貢獻也逐步發酵，26Q2 已來到雙位數水準，預計隨著無人機產品
從單晶片轉往整體模組方案 ， 倍數成長的單價將進一步帶動營收的
增長。此外義隆也透過投資 PIC 設計公司 PETA 來切入矽光子領
域，期望共同開發出下一代 3.2T EIC 產品。綜合以上，研究部預
估義隆 2026 年 EPS 9.50 元，目前 PE 為 16.4x，參考歷史 PE 區
間為 12-24x，考量公司開始往無人機、矽光子領域布局，前者將
開始貢獻營收，後者則會逐步發表新品，讓義隆擺脫過往受 NB 景
氣波動的影響，因此給予 22xPER，目標價 210 元，建議買進。 
⚫ 公司基本資料 ⚫ 公司基本資料 
義隆(2458)成立於 1994 年，為國內晶圓設計廠，產品應用聚焦於
NB 且多為全球領域龍頭 ， 觸控板及觸控螢幕晶片及Point Stick 市
佔率高達 50%以上，2025 年產品組合包含觸控板 46%、觸控螢幕
晶片 22%、 指紋辨識12%、Point Stick 13%、MCU 2%及其他 3%。
銷售地區則為香港 73%、中國 18%、台灣 7%、其他 2%，競爭對
手主要為 Synaptics。

日 週 月 ⚫ 近況更新 
客戶急單挹注，26Q1 淡季不淡 
義隆 26Q1 營收 32.2 億元 ，Q+12.8%，Y+3.4%， 毛利率47.63%，
稅後淨利 7.1 億元，Q-4.2%，Y+28.6%，EPS 2.46 元。零組件的
短缺和記憶體成本的上漲，讓客戶加急下單， 營收表現呈現淡季不
淡。然而產品組合的變化也讓整體毛利率略為下滑，費用則有部分
政府的補助讓整體本業獲利較上季度成長。

AI 產品貢獻逾雙位數，產品組合有望轉佳 
義隆 5 月營收 10.0 億元，M-12.0%，Y+3.6%，4 月營收 11.4 億
元，M-6.7%，Y+6.3%，4-5 月合計 21.5 億元，Y+5.0%。客戶端
持續增加零組件的庫存備貨，同時 AI 影像辨識產品的營收貢獻也
逐步拉高，4-5 月合計貢獻營收 11%，因此公司展望 26Q2 營收維
持 32-34 億元水準 ， 毛利率則預期產品組合相對改善下 ， 預估區間
為 46.5-49.5%，然而光罩費用及展覽等支出也讓整體費用水準提
升，因此預估 OPM 區間為 21.4-25.9%。綜合以上，研究部預估
義隆 26Q2 營收 33.4 億元 ，Q+3.5%，Y+10.1%， 毛利率48.13%，
稅後淨利 6.7 億元，Q-4.6%，Y+90.1%，EPS 2.21 元。

無人機產品發酵，切入矽光子領域 
自 25Q3 起義隆 AI 影像辨識產品已開始正式量產出貨，營收貢獻
也在上半年提升至雙位數占比，除了車用 ADAS 產品外，無人機
的 ISP 晶片貢獻也將跟隨與無人機廠雷虎及其他合作夥伴出貨增
加， 此外義隆產品也將從單晶片轉往 Camera 模組甚至整個通訊模
組，產品單價將為數倍成長，年底更有望開始往三光鏡頭酬載產品
發展 ， 產品單價又將會再往另一水準躍升 ， 另外地面飛控站( GCS )
也會從面板模組到含指紋辨識、陣列天線等整組方案出貨，期望在
非紅供應鏈趨勢下，全球年百萬架的無人機需求帶動出貨的增長。
-1.26 -6.29 3.3

3 
另外義隆也將切往矽光領域，透過投資 PETA Optroni cs，義隆將
開始研發下一代 3.2T EIC 產品，目前PETA Optronics 已有 800G
及 1.6T PIC 產品，因此研究部正向看待公司在未來透過與 PIC 設
計公司的合作共同推出 OE 產品來切入矽光子領域 。 至於在NB 產
品方面，由於上半年客戶的急單拉貨，使營收表現呈現淡季不淡，
然而公司同時也時刻觀察客戶庫存水準來調整出貨節奏 ， 以免重回
先前 Overbooking 的情況，另外由於上游代工和封測廠成本的上
漲，公司預計將於 7 月開始漲價，同樣有助於產品獲利率的提升。
綜合以上，研究部預估義隆 2026 年營收 135.4 億元，Y+9.8%，
毛利率 48.02%，稅後淨利 28.9 億元，Y+18.2%，EPS 9.50 元。

結論 
上半年客戶的急單讓營收呈現淡季不淡，同時 AI 影像辨識產品的
貢獻也逐步發酵，26Q2 已來到雙位數水準，預計隨著無人機產品
從單晶片轉往整體模組方案 ， 倍數成長的單價將進一步帶動營收的
增長。此外義隆也透過投資 PIC 設計公司 PETA 來切入矽光子領
域，期望共同開發出下一代 3.2T EIC 產品。綜合以上，研究部預
估義隆 2026 年 EPS 9.50 元，目前 PE 為 16.4x，參考歷史 PE 區
間為 12-24x，考量公司開始往無人機、矽光子領域布局，前者將
開始貢獻營收，後者則會逐步發表新品，讓義隆擺脫過往受NB 景
氣波動的影響，因此給予 22xPER，目標價 210 元，建議買進。

King Slide (2059.TW): Mgmt. visit: Rail kits expansion in AI data center;
Positive on margin sustainability and growth; Buy
1 June 2026 | 11:55AM HKT
We hosted King Slide management in Taipei on June 1 during our Taiwan Corporate
Day. Management remains positive on its growth driven by growing AI servers trend.

We remain positive on King Slide on (1) its leading market position in AI servers rail
kits, backed by a comprehensive customer base and diversified ar chitecture, which
support extensive customization and protect margins, (2) the rising rail kits adoption
in AI data center, expanding from computing rack to networking rack / CPO switch
rack, power rack, etc., (3) its highly automated production and strong cost controls
due to large scale, supporting the company’s margins level. Maintain Buy.

Key takeaways: Management remains positive on the company’s leading market
position, highlighting their strong patents and early participants across various
architecture (e.g. GPU, ASIC, HGX, DGX, MGX, OCP , etc.). King Slide’s diversified
architecture, strong customization capabilities, and early participation in the design
stage (e.g. end users are used to King Slide’s interface, easy to adopt, etc.) help
secure its leading market position and margins. Management highlighted that, while

rail kits a
ccount for only a small portion of AI servers total BOM costs, they are a
critical component in ensuring AI servers smooth operation and maintenance. As a
result, end customers prioritize product quality rather than ASP . The company
remains committed to capacity expansion, based on April 2026 revenues, the
company’s utilization rate is at 50%, and will add additional capacity in the US in
3Q26, and further expansion in Taiwan in 2H27.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - King Slide
Valuation methodology: Our 12m target price for King Slide is based on a
near-term P/E, consistent with our Taiwan Technology coverage. Our 12m TP of
NT$4,386 is based on a target P/E multiple of 20x on our forward year EPS (2027E).
Our target P/E is derived from the company’s peak PEG at 0.8x, as we expect the
migration to AI server rail kits to drive the company’s profitability
.

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a B uy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not C overed), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region ’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the r eturn across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment R eview Committee or

other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such s tocks.
Total return potential represents the upside or downside differ ential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all cov ered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership .

Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at
https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

### Page 1

Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C., Taipei
Branch
Zhen Ding Technology Holding (4958.TW): Taiwan Computex &
Corporate Day: Multi-year expansion to support strong AI
1 June 2026 | 10:35PM CST
We hosted meetings with Zhen Ding at our Taiwan Computex & Corporate Day
(June 1st). Key takeaways include:
(1) Upward revision on IC substrate/AI server/optical business: Management has
revised up its guidance on IC substrate/AI server/optical business to reach 40-45% of

total revenue by 2030 (previous guidance was 20-25%), and expects these
businesses to reach 25% in 2026 (was ~12% in 2025).
(2) IC substrate business with strong momentum: Management has revised up its
IC substrate business YoY growth from 60% to 80% in 2026 with strong visibility
extended into 2027. ABF substrate business has turned profitable in 1Q26, while ABF
plant 2 in ShenZhen will begin contributing from 2H26 and the company will
continue to expand non-China ABF substrate capacity.

(3) Ongoing progress with mSAP solutions for 1.6T optical: The company is
currently sampling mSAP solutions for 1.6T optical and expects to reach MP starting
from 3Q26. Optical revenue is expected to account for 4% of total revenue in 2026
(vs. 0.4% in 2025). Management mentioned that yield is currently at 80%+, and is
improving toward 90%, and believes the leading yield vs. peers is the key reason that
the company can capture leading market share in optical mSAP solutions amid

supply shortage.
(4) Massive multi-year CAPEX cycles supported by customer demand:
Management reiterated its 2026 CAPEX guidance of NT$80bn, driven by customer
orders and long-term agreements. This year’s CAPEX is primarily for expanding HDI
and higher layer count PCBs (>65% of CAPEX), with the remainder allocated between
IC substrates (15-20%) and FPC (~15%). The company also mentioned CAPEX
spending is expected to remain high at similar levels throughout 2028-30.

(5) Consumer business remains stable while mSAP shifts toward AI: The
consumer business remains core, but as IC substrate/AI server/optical scale up,
overall seasonality in GM is expected to flatten in 2026 (was seeing 80ppts quarterly
GM variation in 2025). mSAP was previously mainly used in smartphones, but is
expected to shift toward a 50:50 split between smartphone and optical/AI server in
next 3 years driven by the strong demand.
(6) IC substrate subsidiary going public: Zhen Ding’s IPO plan for its IC substrate

subsidiary, Leading Tech, has passed board approval, with documentation to be
delivered in 2H26, followed by a planned IPO in Hong Kong in 2027.
Goldman Sachs does and seeks to do business with companies covered in its research reports. As a result, investors
should be aware that the firm ma y have a conflict of interest that could affect the objectivity of this report.
Investors should consider this report as only a single factor in making their investment decision. For Reg AC

www.gs.com/research/hedge.html
. Analysts employed by non-US affiliates are not registered/qualified as research
analysts with FINRA in the U.S.

### Page 2

Outlook
We are positive on ZDT’s IC substrate/AI server/optical business outlook as management
guided for these businesses to contribute 25% of total revenue in 2026. We expect AI
server demand to a
ccelerate in 2H26, supported by the company’s successful PCB
verifica tion for Vera Rubin’s compute tray, and we expect the contribution to become
more meaningful in 2H26-1H27, with better profitability (a
verage OPM of 20-30% for AI
computing tray vs. company overall OPM of 10-15%), assuming the company continues

to deliver good yield. Furthermore, we expect to see increasing contribution from both
ASIC-related projects (from AWS and Google) and optical solutions (with PCB for 1.6T
optical module to see small volume starting in 2Q26) in 2026. We expect to see more
ABF substrate contribution upon the completion of ShenZhen Plant 2 and the company
continues to expand non-China capacity.
The company reiterated its 2026 CAPEX guidance of NT$80bn+ (implying 150%+ YoY),

as the company expects to complete 10 factories this year as part of its long-term
strategy to build 15 factories. The company guided that 65% of 2026 CAPEX will be
allocated for the server and optical business, 15% to the IC substrate business, and 10%
to the PFC business. The proactive investment plan is in line with our positive view that
AI PCB will see 140% 2025-27E CAGR (see here
), and we believe ZDT is well-positioned
to be one of the winners in the AI era considering its strong execution track record and

proactive capacity expansion plan relative to peers.
Valuation: Our 12-month TP of NT$388 is based on a SOTP valuation methodology,
including (1) 2.6x blended 4Q26-3Q27E P/B for ABF & BT substrate, (2) 20x
4Q26-3Q27E P/E for high-growth AI server/switch related PCB (in line with industry
average), and (3) 8x 4Q26-3Q27E P/E for low-growth consumer electronics PCB (in-line
with industry downcycle average valuation).
Key downside risks include: (1) weaker-than-expected iPhone demand or slower

content upgrade, (2) delay in new substrate capacity or slower-than-expected yield rate
ramp up progress, and (3) fier
cer competition in China ABF market.

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y
ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a B uy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not C overed), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region ’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the r eturn across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment R eview Committee or

other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such s tocks.
Total return potential represents the upside or downside differ ential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all cov ered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership .

Style Exposure
• MLCC UTR could rise fast in coming months. YAGEO does not have the
leadership in the high capacitance/voltage MLCCs, which are used heavily in

AI server systems. However, as much of its major peers' capacity is tied up with
these fast-growing AI products, which can consume as much as 3-5x
production resources vs regular MLCCs, we expect the overflow orders to lift
YAGEO's IT MLCC UTR by 10ppt in 1H (YoY), and further increased to 15ppt
YoY in 2H (hence we now expect 85-90% peak IT UTR for YAGEO to be
reached in 2H; while auto/industrial MLCC UTR shall also improve on YoY
basis). We also believe that, due to the AI-driven high UTR and B/B ratios of

the industry, the potential consumer demand impact (if the segment weakens)
should be manageable. As a rule of thumb, the key factor behind the pricing of
passive component is the respective UTR of the product.
• General IT MLCC could see a bit of pricing surprise. On the pricing side,
while the major AI MLCC suppliers in Japan and Korea have not finalized the
price hike decisions, the JPM team expects an increasingly constructive pricing
environment. It is crucial to understand that the price hike timing and

momentum for general IT (non-AI) MLCCs could surprise on the upside, as
hikes could take place earlier/rise in stronger magnitude vs the AI MLCCs. We
believe many suppliers would leverage the industry supply tightness to
improve the mix and/or the relatively low margins of these non-AI
applications, where the pricing initiative could start with non-AI products.
Please keep in mind we have seen this situation occurr for memory, PCB and
CCL in the past few years, and this could result in strong incremental

improvements in profitability for general IT MLCCs, a situation that we
believe is favorable for YAGEO.
• AI story much beyond MLCC : Tantalum entering multi-year up-cycle.
We believe YAGEO has a bit of a different AI story vs. its peers, which mainly
produce MLCCs. YAGEO’s revenue mix is largely filled with non-MLCC
products such as Tantalum (No.1 product by rev mix for YAGEO at ~25/30%
in 2026/27e), power inductors (25-30% of YAGEO’s total inductors, which are

its No.2 product by revenue) and resistors (14/15% of YAGEO revenue in
2026/27e). This could see a very rapid rise in AI mix, and/or see stronger
pricing momentum as YAGEO can exert much more clout on pricing vs.
MLCCs, which are largely determined by Japanese producers (which have a
tendency to maintain stable pricing). We expect tantalum’s double-digit ASP
growth in recent years will likely accelerate as AI quickly becomes 35-40% of
its mix (driven by the need for power management and data storage). Investors

Summary Investment Thesis and Valuation
Investment Thesis
We are OW on YAGEO, given its leading position in tantalum
capacitors after acquiring Kemet and its strong presence in
MLCCs/R-Chips, a market that has consolidated over time with
disciplined supply expansion. We believe less exposure to
commodity-type products can help smooth the revenue
fluctuations, while increasing demand for tantalum capacitors

can support YAGEO’s future top- and bottom-line growth.
Valuation
Our Dec-26 PT of NT$1,000 is based on 26x 2027/28E EPS. 26x
is beyond 1.5-STD above average forward P/E at which the
company has traded since 2021. We believe the multiple is
justified as passive component enters demand-driven upcycle,
which is more structural and healthy versus the previous cycle
in 2018-19. We believe YAGEO is one of the key beneficiaries
of this cycle with 1) dominance in high-end passives (global

leader in Tantalum), and 2) MLCC price hike starting from the
lower-end segment.

(behind Tantalum and inductor), has drew much less attention. We believe resistors,
which have a high correlation in usage vs. MLCC, could be a major incremental OP
contributor with comparable impact to Tantalum in the coming years, as we see healthy
pricing upside. YAGEO has quite a dominating global share (40% by volume, which is
a very high level in the world of passive components), while the product has gone through

many years of modest pricing (hence it currently makes up a tiny small portion of the
passive BOM despite its indispensable role), the growing demand now likely pushes
YAGEO’s resistor UTR to north of 90% in the upcoming peak season. Given these
favorable conditions, we expect resistor pricing, which saw a successful hike in 1Q26,
to increase by 15-20% CAGR in the coming years and further improve its already decent
profitability.
• Lifting TP to NT$1,000, based on 26x of average 2027/28e EPS. YAGEO is currently

trading at 48/27x 2026/27e EPS, which is meaningfully below its Japanese and Korean
peers, despite 1) comparably strong earnings growth (with potential upside if price
adjustment becomes stronger vs. our assumptions of ASP showing a ~15% CAGR in the
coming 2-3 years), and 2) similar exposure to AI/HPC (but with much more diverse
product exposure). Our new target multiple, at 26x (was 19x which was above 1.5-STD
above mean; previous TP based on 2027e), is slightly above the 23-24x forward earnings

multiple YAGEO traded at during the 2018-19 peak cycle. While there are some
similarities between the previous cycle vs the current one (EV/industrial caused supply
tightness vs. AI) we believe such re-rating is deserving as this margin expansion cycle
could last even longer as most of the major suppliers seem to have taken mix improvement
as their long term goal, which leads more rational capacity expansions. We also believe
the AI driven demand will moderate the pace of component size miniaturization, hence

positive to the industry supply/demand balance.

Investment Thesis, Valuation and Risks
YAGEO (Overweight; Price Target: NT$1,000.00)
Investment Thesis
We are OW on YAGEO, given its leading position in tantalum capacitors after acquiring
Kemet and its strong presence in MLCCs/R-Chips, a market that has consolidated over time
with disciplined supply expansion. We believe less exposure to commodity-type products

can help smooth the revenue fluctuations, while increasing demand for tantalum capacitors
can support YAGEO’s future top- and bottom-line growth.
Valuation
Our Dec-26 PT of NT$1,000 is based on 26x 2027/28E EPS. 26x is beyond 1.5-STD above
average forward P/E at which the company has traded since 2021. We believe the multiple
is justified as passive component enters demand-driven upcycle, which is more structural
and healthy versus the previous cycle in 2018-19. We believe YAGEO is one of the key

beneficiaries of this cycle with 1) dominance in high-end passives (global leader in
Tantalum), and 2) MLCC price hike starting from the lower-end segment.
Risks to Rating and Price Target
Downside risks include MLCC/R-Chip price reversals (which could be driven by a slower
pace of auto electrification, inventory corrections, etc.) and execution issues during
mergers.

Explanation of Equity Research Ratings, Designations and Analyst(s) Coverage Universe:
J.P. Morgan uses the following rating system: Overweight (over the duration of the price target indicated in this report, we expect this stock will

outperform the average total return of the stocks in the Research Analyst’s, or the Research Analyst’s team’s, coverage universe); Neutral (over
the duration of the price target indicated in this report, we expect this stock will perform in line with the average total return of the stocks in the
Research Analyst’s, or the Research Analyst’s team’s, coverage universe); and Underweight (over the duration of the price target indicated in
this report, we expect this stock will underperform the average total return of the stocks in the Research Analyst’s, or the Research Analyst’s

team’s, coverage universe. NR is Not Rated. In this case, J.P. Morgan has removed the rating and, if applicable, the price target, for this stock
because of either a lack of a sufficient fundamental basis or for legal, regulatory or policy reasons. The previous rating and, if applicable, the
price target, no longer should be relied upon. An NR designation is not a recommendation or a rating. Some stocks under coverage have a rating
but no price target; in these cases, we expect the stock will outperform/perform in line/underperform the average total return of the stocks in the

the certifying Research Analyst’s coverage universe can be found on J.P. Morgan’s Research website, https://www.jpmorganmarkets.com.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
AP Memory Technology Corp (6531.TW)
Base case, derived from a residual income model. Key RI model assumptions include 1) cost
of equity constant at 9.2% (2.0% risk-free rate, 6% risk premium, 1.2 beta); 2) payout ratio of
67%; 3) terminal growth rate of 3.0%; and 4) medium-term growth rate of 16.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected consumer demand
n Faster-than-expected IPD ramp and WoW packaging development

n Milder competition from partners (foundry, memory house, GPU vendor) or other
design houses
Risks to Downside
n Weaker-than-expected consumer demand
n Slower-than-expected IPD ramp and WoW packaging development
n Intensifying competition from partners (foundry, memory house, GPU vendor) or other
design houses

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.

M
Update
WinWay Technology Co Ltd  | Asia Pacific
Asia   AI Summit 2026 Feedback
Larger package size drives testing interface demand: As testing time continues to
increase, the pin count per socket has increased from 4-6k to over 10k, and
management expects this trend to  continue in the next few years as chips become
more and more complex. Incremental testing steps, such as functional burn-in or
SL T, also expand the TAM for Winway.
Burn-in socket:  This was not the key focus for Winway in the past, but it said it will

now try
to target the high-end segment instead of dealing with pricing competition
in the low-end market once it expands its  new capacity in the next few years.
Capacity expansion plan: Winway expects its pogo pin capacity for test sockets to
reach 9mn/month by end-2026, then 14mn/month by 1H27, mainly from its Renwu
Facility 1. Renwu Facility 2 is expected to start in 2Q27, further lifting capacity to
14mn/month. The current pogo-pin self-sufficiency ratio is ~35-45%; management

Valuation Methodology and Risks
WinWay Technology Co Ltd (6515.TW)
We derive our price target from a residual income model, as we believe this can best capture
long-term value. We assume a 9.14% cost of equity (risk-free rate 2.0% and risk premium
5.5%), a payout ratio of 80%, a medium-term growth rate of 16.4%, and a terminal growth rate
of 4.5%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected adoption of hypersockets and MEMS probe cards

n Faster-than-expected SL T and burn-in socket introduction
n Stronger-than-expected edge AI demand
Risks to Downside
n Slower-than-expected adoption of hypersockets and MEMS probe cards
n Slower-than-expected SL T and burn-in socket introduction
n No edge AI demand in the next few years

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

Taiwan Computex & Corporate Day: Ta Liang (3167.TW, NC); Increasing
PCB production difficulty , suggesting more industry testing/drilling
2 June 2026 | 9:59PM CST
We hosted meetings with Ta Liang (3167.TW, Not Covered), which is the key
high-end PCB production/testing equipment supplier in Taiwan, at our Taiwan
Computex & Corporate Day (June 1st). Key takeaways include:
(1) Increasing next-gen AI PCB production difficulty is driving

testing/measurement equipment demand: Management estimated the TM
(measurement) machine to PCB back-drilling machine ratio should be 1:8 in the
beginning of this year, but it starts seeing 1:6 design (vs. previous guidance of only
1:8) from customers, due mainly to the decreasing production yield for the next-gen
AI PCB, which, per our view, should imply a solid industry investment on this
testing/measurement equipment in coming quarters/years, considering the

increasing complexity of the PCB production process.
(2) Strong demand for 1.6T optical transceiver mSAP production equipment: The
company mentioned the demand outlook for 1.6T optical transceiver mSAP routing
solutions is strong, implying a solid PCB demand in coming quarters/years. Despite
that the final design of the 1.6T optical transceiver PCB routing equipment is not yet
finalized, and the company mentioned it has the dominant position in PCB routing

equipment with much higher than 50% market share, and is confident on its market
share in the 1.6T optical transceiver PCB equipment market.
(3) Capacity remains tight with full utilization, and outsourcing strategy and
de-bottleneck could further increase product shipment: Management mentioned
the current order is fully booked for the next 9 months while the effective monthly
capacity is ~200-300 units now (vs. previous guidance of 800 units of high end

machines). However, the company has no further plan to build new capacity, but it
will start outsourcing more low end product production to its outsourcing partner
from end of 2026 (plan to outsource ~50 units of low end equipment per month), to
work on more high end equipment in coming years. Moreover, the company
mentioned it is also pursuing efficiency improvements, with potential to achieve a
10% capacity uplift internally, although the company did not rule out the possibility

of capacity expansion through acquisitions, to have access to specialized labor.
(4) Semi-equipment business is scaling but still early phase: The company
mentioned it continues to make progress in semi inspection equipment, with 7 of its
solutions adopted in CoWoS and 2 in CoPoS with ASP ranging from low mn to the
teens of mn of NT$. The company is seeing OSAT demand rising (to account ~70% of
semi mix in 2026 vs. 50% in 2025), and the company is engaging with non-Taiwan

Read across to PCB suppliers: The company mentioned strong demand in 1.6T optical,
which is in line with the outlook from optical transceiver PCB suppliers (see here), while
we continue to expect a better adoption of the high-end mSAP technology, suggesting a
better outlook for the key mSAP suppliers. Management highlighted an improvement in
the TM to back-drilling machine ratio, which we believe is driven by higher PCB
measurement demand as the PCB yields remains low, and we believe the further

investment in PCB CAPEX could be expected from the industry in coming
quarters/years. We expect TM machine could become a potential bottleneck as demand
driven by low PCB yields may already outpace supply.

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y
ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a B uy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not C overed), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region ’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the r eturn across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment R eview Committee or

other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such s tocks.
Total return potential represents the upside or downside differ ential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all cov ered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership .

Airtac International Group (1590.TW): May Monthly revenue -2% vs.
GSe; New orders continue to outpace shipments
3 June 2026 | 9:25PM CST
On June 2nd, AirTAC reported May revenue of Rmb844mn (-10% MoM, +26% YoY)
/ NT$3,909mn (-10% MoM, +40% YoY), which was 2% above GSe in Rmb terms.
In May, AirTAC’s revenue was 2% below GSe in Rmb terms, down 10% MoM with 9%
fewer working days, mainly due to the May holidays in Mainland China. This miss was

primarily due to (1) fewer working days MoM (20.5 days in May 2026 vs. 22.5 days in
April 2026), (2) electronics and battery showing double digit MoM decline, outpace
the actual decline in workings days, and (3) weaker than expected linear guide
revenue contribution (Rmb54mn missed GSe of Rmb62mn by 13%).
Moreover, May revenue was up 26% YoY (in Rmb) with the same working days as May
2025. We believe this solid YoY revenue growth was driven by (1) strong battery

demand, which continued to deliver strong YoY revenue growth over the past few
quarters, (2) YoY growth across nearly all end markets reached double digit YoY as
China pneumatic demand environment continue to improve. However, revenue
contribution from linear guides was lower in May at Rmb54mn vs. GSe of Rmb62mn
vs. Rmb60mn in April, but the daily average linear guides revenue remains high at
Rmb2.6mn (vs. Rmb2.7mn in April), as AirTAC’s linear guide products maintained

strong price competitiveness (c.20-30% discount vs. peers) and the company
expects the linear guide utilization rate to trend up sequentially throughout 2026
(see here
).
In terms of revenue growth by end-market segment for March (see Exhibit 1), the
battery sector continued to outgrow all other sectors (+55% YoY), suggesting
sustained demand. Key segments’ monthly revenue YoY trend includes textile (+51%
YoY), machine tool (35% YoY), general machinery (35% YoY), packaging (+20%),

impact remains manageable and continues to enhance internal production efficiency
and optimize its product mix. Moreover, the company continues to maintain a stable

Our Asia Industrial team highlighted strengthening demand across FA industry (see
here), while supply constraints emerging as the primary bottleneck. FA equipment and

some FA components are experiencing order pull-ins. This is consistent with AirTAC’s

Also, we believe AirTAC’s strong B/S control and healthy cash flo
w will help the company
to overcome any potential industry demand risk, and at the same time, help the

company to enjoy a stronger share gaining process during demand downtrends (AirTAC’s
share gain progress has a
ccelerated during industry downcycles, due to its solid B/S and
cash flo w control). We expect the company’s ROE will continue to rise in the coming
years (ROE should improve to 24.8% in 2028E per GSe vs. 16%-17% in 2022-24)
supporting a high dividend payout ratio (50%-60%+ per year per GSe) in the foreseeable
future.
Maintain Buy on AirTAC with an unchanged 12m TP of NT$1,725.

Investment Thesis - AirTAC
AirTAC is the 2nd-largest pneumatic supplier in mainland China with 25% market share
in 2024, outpacing most local competitors, owing to its low-cost yet high quality
product, and service quality reputation in most key cities in mainland China. We forecast
its market share will reach 31% in 2026E and expect AirTAC to be the structural winner
in a defla tionary market, due to its: (1) strong market growth and leadership: AirTAC

continues to expand its market share in mainland China driven by competitive pricing
that is more than 10% lower vs. international peers (with 45%+ GM / 25-30% OPM) and
high demand elasticity among mainland China customers; and (2) superior cost structure
and financial health: with bes
t-in-class cash cost and a solid balance sheet, featuring
positive net cash over FCF since 2023/2024, we believe AirTAC is well-equipped to
weather industry downturns and capitalize on market restructuring.

Overall, we believe AirTAC will see better revenue/profitability in the long t erm given its
share gainer position in the China pneumatic market and potential to gain share in the
linear guide market (improved demand in the next 1-2 years). This should lead to
strengthened valuation, vs. its recent trading range (trading below its 10-year avg. P/E
multiple). We are Buy rated on AirTAC.
Price target risks and methodology - AirTAC
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$1,725 is based on a target P/E of 24.2x on

our 3Q26E-2Q27E EPS (avg of the last pneumatic upcycle).
Key downside risks: (1) Stronger competition from peers; (2) sharper-than-expected
Rmb depreciation, and (3) a longer-than-expected end-market demand downtrend in
mainland China.

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y
ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a B uy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not C overed), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region ’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the r eturn across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment R eview Committee or

other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such s tocks.

We remain positive on Gigabyte’s AI server business and expect a
39% CAGR for the company’s AI server revenues in 2025-28E, with

AI servers revenue contribution up to 74% in 2028E (vs. 54% in
2025). The company’s April revenues were up 74% YoY, or 51%
higher than our previous estimates on AI server ramp-up, and we
expect the strong momentum to continue, driven by (1) rack-level AI
server specifica
tion upgrade to drive dollar content upgrade, (2)
server capacity expansion to support volume ramp-up, and (3) AI
total solution helping clients to deploy AI with higher efficienc

y.
Maintain Neutral with higher NT$487 TP .
AI server business ramp-up: The company is capable of developing
AI servers across differ ent architecture, with GB300 NVL72 in
production, and Vera Rubin platforms under development.
Meanwhile, Gigabyte also provides integrated solution incl. the
pre-integrated AI Pod (GIGAPOD) for high-density GPU cluster
deployment, and the united GPM platform on monitoring,
scheduling, and resource management. We see the company’s total

solution helps to a
ccelerate clients’ AI deployment. We expect to see
AI server business growth with specifica tion upgrade towards new
platform and capacity ramp up.
Monthly revenues preview: Gigabyte Apr 2026 revenues were up
74% YoY/ 34% MoM to NT$52.3bn, or 51% beat our previous
estimates, and we attribute it to stronger-than-expected AI server
growth across the HGX and GB platform. Looking ahead, we expect
the company’s 2Q26E revenue to grow at 26% YoY (or 22% MoM) to

NT$129bn, with MoM decrease of May/ Jun revenues post the Apr
pull-in. We expect the rising AI server revenue contribution to
support growth ahead.

### Page 3

Earnings revision: We raise NI by 36%/ 26%/ 29% in 2026-28E on higher revenues,
reflec ting our positive view on (1) the company’s capabilities to deliver AI server across
various architecture, benefiting fr om the dollar content upgrade, (2) capacity ramp-up
to support volume ramp-up, and (3) comprehensive ecosystem: offering the
pre-integrated AI Pod (GIGAPOD) to enhance the efficienc y of high-density GPU cluster
deployment. We raise 2026 GM on mix upgrade towards high-end solution and cost

management, while lower 2027/ 28E GM by 0.2ppts/ 0.2ppts to reflec t higher revenue
contribution from rack-level server with lower margin.
GS vs. consensus: Our earnings estimates in 2026/ 27E are largely in line with
consensus. Our 2026E revenue estimate is 3% lower than consensus due to lower
revenues of graphic card and motherboard, while our revenue estimate in 2027E is 1%
higher than consensus on positive outlook of AI server revenues, with lower GM due to

lower margin of rack AI server business.

Valuation: We continue to use near-term P/E to derive our 12M TP . Our 2027E target

target P/E of 16.8x is in-line with the company’s historical trading range and in line with
company’s avg. +1std P/E at 16x since 2010. Maintain Neutral.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Gigabyte
We are Neutral rated on Gigabyte with a 12-month target price of NT$487. Our TP is
based on a 16.8x 2027E P/E which is based on PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s share
performance.
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market
recovery, 2) faster-/slower-than-expected gaming PC growth, and 3)
faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up.

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Strong Preliminary April Profits
Asustek reported April 2026 profit numbers per TWSE regulatory requirements.
• April 2026 revenue was NT$84.9bn.
• April 2026 NP was NT$5,313mn (+283% y/y), which accounts for 42% and
50% of our and the Street's 2Q26 NP estimates, respectively.
•  NpM was 6.3%

beat and provided strong 2Q26 guidance for its PC (+10-15% q/q) and server

businesses (+50% q/q).
This aligns with Lenovo's comment that PC pull-ins will persist into 2Q (
report),
though Asustek's OpM guidance appeared to be overly conservative at only 4-5%,
vs, April NpM of 6.3%..

Key Takeaways
Asustek's April profits imply upside risks to both our 2Q estimates and the
Street's.
However, we hold to our view that the market will repay the pulled-forward
demand sometime in 2H...
...when PC prices rise more meaningfully for consumers, which will eventually
lead to demand destruction.
We also think this is priced in with the stock up 51%  over the past month (the

TAIEX is up 12% over the same period).
Within our Greater China PC OEM coverage, we have a relative preference for
Lenovo (EW) over Asustek (UW) and Acer (UW).

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Lenovo (0992.HK)
Base case, residual income model. Our key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.3% (beta
of 1.1, risk-free rate of 2.0%), a medium-term growth rate of 5.0%, and a terminal growth rate
of 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected smartphone and data center turnaround
n Stronger PC demand recovery in China
n Better cost savings and improved product mix
n Lower-than-expected increase in memory price

Risks to Downside
n Slower-than-expected smartphone and data center turnaround
n Weaker PC demand recovery in China
n Deterioration in cost savings and product mix
n Higher-than-expected increase in memory price
Acer Inc. (2353.TW)
Base case, residual income model. We use RI model for Acer's valuation. Key assumptions
include a cost of equity of 9.2% (beta of 0.9, equity risk premium of 10.2% and risk-free rate
of 0.5%), a 6.0% medium-term growth rate, and a 3% terminal growth rate.

Risks to Upside
n Stronger macro recovery than expected
n Share gains in the PC market
n Better-than-expected cost controls
n Lower-than-expected increase in memory cost
Risks to Downside
n Weaker macro recovery than expected
n Share loss in the PC market
n Worse-than-expected cost controls
n Higher-than-expected increase in memory cost
Asustek Computer Inc. (2357.TW)
Base case, sum of the parts:
We apply a 9x multiple to Asustek's 2026 brand profits. This is somewhat below the midpoint

of its historical band of 7-15x since 2009, which we view as fair because memory prices are
rising and will have a negative impact on margins.
We value its stakes in Pegatron and Advantech based on our price targets in proportion to
Asustek's holdings.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Greater China Technology Hardware

Industry View In-Line
Accelerated VR rack shipment deliveries: The implementation of automation
equipment and AI algorithms is helping to shorten the cycle time of Vera Rubin
server rack system assembly. For referenced,  the GTC Taipei keynote address cited
that VR computing tray assembly time has been reduced to five minutes from 2
hours for GB computing trays. This is key to enhancing time-to-volume deliveries.
AI server momentum keeps growing: GB300 server rack order visibility at Foxconn

is till the end of next year. Foxconn aims to have VR NVL72 server racks in mass
production by the end of 3Q26 and expects VR racks to become mainstream in
1Q27. AI ASIC server rack delivery will also increase, with contribution increasing to
30% of AI server revenue in 2H26.
Sovereign AI fuels long-term growth: Foxconn will participate in several sovereign
AI projects with more than US$1trn in AI investment  over the next five years,
including recently announced deals in France. Foxconn is dominant in the sovereign

AI market given its leading  position in AI computing system integration. It  will likely
help build local ecosystems in target markets.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)
Base case scenario value, derived from a residual income valuation model. Our key assump-
tions include a cost of equity of 8.5%, a medium-term growth rate of 13%, and a terminal
growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected iPhone sell-through
n Faster progress in AI server business
n Faster EV business development progress
n Any new M&A activity that could improve sentiment

Risks to Downside
n Lower iPhone sell-through
n Smaller profit contribution from AI business
n Slower EV business development progress
n Geopolitical developments that could negatively affect foreign investment
Foxconn Industrial Internet Co. Ltd. (601138.SS)
Base case, derived from a multi-stage residual income (RI) valuation model. Key assumptions:
n A 10% cost of equity, in line with our tech hardware coverage universe.
n A medium-term growth rate of 18%, reflecting the benefits of its ongoing investment,

enriched solution offerings and multi-year AI demand opportunity.
n A terminal growth rate of 5%.
Risks to Upside
n Faster project win progress in IIoT solutions
n Lower competition in its manufacturing business
n Faster-than-expected 5G and data center demand
n Stronger macro outlook
Risks to Downside
n Slow project win progress in IIoT solutions
n Increased competition in its manufacturing business
n Lower-than-expected 5G and data center demand

n Weaker macro outlook
